# Revision v6 — six-hour goal

Window: 2026-10-23 18:31:50 UTC to 2026-10-24 00:31:50 UTC (Vancouver 11:31:50–17:31:50 on 23 October). Status: active, WP1. Self-check every 600 seconds. Do not mark the complete goal achieved before the deadline.

## Boundaries and rulings

- User-specified checkout and branch supply isolation; do not create another worktree or alter main.
- No repository AGENTS.md or parent filesystem AGENTS.md was present; current user-chat AGENTS instructions govern.
- No installation or deletion, including skill cleanup. Use existing runtime/temporary uv dependencies only.
- The local v5 branch and UI-published remote have distinct histories but verified identical scoped file bytes; preserve both histories.
- At v5 closure there was no same-head PR; verify again before final publication and do not overwrite the other supervisor PR.
- Plan claims about ACI, CAViaR interpretation and conditional predictive ability require primary-source and applicability verification before implementation.
- All new analyses are post-result/exploratory; no original primary or frozen empirical result will be replaced.

## Priority queue

WP1 internal self-review → WP2 delayed ACI / headroom SAV → WP3 fixed combinations → WP4 state/conditional predictive ability → WP5 quantile levels → WP6 integration → WP7 language and full verification. Each new analysis design must be committed before financial output; each WP ends with a commit, commands and literal outputs. After all WPs use only the authorized audit loop.

## Pending author decisions

- None yet beyond the inherited missing same-head PR and formal publication choices.

## WP1 in progress — source and method diagnosis

Three independent same-model contexts review forecasting/evaluation, financial measurement and editorial fit. These are internal self-review documents, not external peer review. Source checking has reopened Gibbs–Candès NeurIPS2021 (immediate feedback and untruncated-state guarantee), Engle–Manganelli July2002 author draft (SAV/optimization; formal2004 publication remains distinct), and Giacomini–White2006 (multistep conditional moments, fixed-memory and covariance assumptions).

Initial issues: separate original-primary Murphy diagrams from matched-history score comparisons; distinguish more breaches from established worse calibration; fix main-table reading order and citation routing; retain source sampling/vintage/economic-value boundaries. All proposed edits await the complete response ledger. No new financial benchmark has run.

Automation: reused paused same-paper heartbeat v3 with the current v6 queue and ten-minute cadence. First attempt lacked the thread destination and was rejected without updating; corrected call succeeded. No polling interval is counted as research work.

Both unchanged original primary loss vectors exactly reproduce archived means and direction before analysis. The six group rows, all four raw/adjusted tests, normalizers, n/burn-in/bandwidth/rank/condition diagnostics and source resource receipts are in wp4-states.json. FX group means favor QR in each of the three training-defined states, but the equal-group-means reference does not strongly support a state difference.
